73-00-00-040-801-F00MMEL 73-10 (DDPG) Preparation - Fuel Control, ENG VALVE CLOSED Indicating SystemPDF p. 17

2. MMEL 73-10 (DDPG) Preparation - Fuel Control, ENG VALVE CLOSED Indicating System
A. General
(1) This task gives the maintenance steps that prepare the airplane for flight with the ENG VALVE
CLOSED light inoperative.
(2) This is the condition for this task:
(a) The two ENG VALVE CLOSED lights may be inoperative provided that the High Pressure
Shutoff Valve (HPSOV) will operate correctly.
B. References
Reference Title
71-00-00-800-807-F00 Start the Engine Procedure (Selection) (P/B 201)
C. Location Zones
Zone Area
211 Flight Compartment - Left
212 Flight Compartment - Right
D. Engine Fuel HPSOV Operational Test
SUBTASK 73-00-00-860-006-F00
(1) For Engine 1, open this circuit breaker and install safety tag:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
E 6 C01395 ENGINE FUEL ENGINE 1 HPSOV IND
SUBTASK 73-00-00-860-007-F00
(2) For Engine 2, open this circuit breaker and install safety tag:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
E 4 C01396 ENGINE FUEL ENGINE 2 HPSOV IND
SUBTASK 73-00-00-860-001-F00
(3) Do these steps to make sure that the HPSOV operates correctly:
73-00-00
(a) Start the applicable engine, do this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Selection),
TASK 71-00-00-800-807-F00.
1) Let the engine become stable at idle.
(b) For Engine 1, open this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
B 4 C00359 FUEL SPAR VALVE ENG 1
(c) For Engine 2, open this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
B 3 C00360 FUEL SPAR VALVE ENG 2
(d) Put the applicable the start lever to the CUTOFF position.
(e) Make sure that the engine immediately begins to decelerate and continues to decelerate
at the usual rate to a complete stop.
NOTE: If the engine does not immediately begin to decelerate, then there is a problem in
the HPSOV system.
1) If the engine does not decelerate correctly, then do these steps:
a) For Engine 1, close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
B 4 C00359 FUEL SPAR VALVE ENG 1
b) For Engine 2, close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
B 3 C00360 FUEL SPAR VALVE ENG 2
c) Do the fault isolation for fault code 730 050 51 (Eng 1) or 730 050 52 (Eng 2).
(f) Make sure that the Start Lever is in the CUTOFF position.
(g) For Engine 1, close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
B 4 C00359 FUEL SPAR VALVE ENG 1
(h) For Engine 2, close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
B 3 C00360 FUEL SPAR VALVE ENG 2

3. MMEL 73-10 (DDPG) Restoration - Fuel Control, ENG VALVE CLOSED Indicating System
A. General
(1) This task puts the airplane back to its usual condition after operation with the ENG VALVE
CLOSED Light, LX1, inoperative.
B. Location Zones
Zone Area
211 Flight Compartment - Left
212 Flight Compartment - Right
C. ENG VALVE CLOSED Light Repair
SUBTASK 73-00-00-860-004-F00
(1) For engine 1, do this step;
Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
E 6 C01395 ENGINE FUEL ENGINE 1 HPSOV IND
SUBTASK 73-00-00-860-005-F00
(2) For engine 2, do this step;
Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker:
F/O Electrical System Panel, P6-3
Row Col Number Name
E 4 C01396 ENGINE FUEL ENGINE 2 HPSOV IND
SUBTASK 73-00-00-810-001-F00
(3) Do these steps to correct the fault:
(a) Find the fault code, or the description of the fault that occurred.
(b) If you find a fault code, then do these steps:
1) Go to the Fault Code Index in the applicable chapter of the FIM, and find the fault
code.
NOTE: The first two digits of the fault code are the FIM chapter.
2) Find the task number on the same line as the fault code.
3) Go to the task in the FIM and do the steps in the task.
(c) If you find a description of the fault, then do these steps:
1) Go to the Observed Fault List at the start of the FIM, and find the best description
for the fault.
2) Find the task number on the same line as the fault description.
3) Go to the task in the FIM, and do the steps in the task.
73-00-00-200-801-F00MMEL 73-4 (DDPG) - Fuel Filter Differential Pressure Warning SystemPDF p. 20
Open original AMM PDF - page 20 ↗ 
4. MMEL 73-4 (DDPG) - Fuel Filter Differential Pressure Warning System
A. General
(1) This task gives the maintenance steps when the fuel filter differential pressure warning system
is inoperative.
B. References
Reference Title
73-11-02-000-801-F00 Fuel Filter - Removal (P/B 401)
73-11-02-400-801-F00 Fuel Filter - Installation (P/B 401)
73-21-00-700-804-F00 EEC TEST (P/B 501)
73-21-00-740-803-F00 EEC BITE TEST - RECENT FAULTS (P/B 501)
C. Location Zones
Zone Area
211 Flight Compartment - Left
212 Flight Compartment - Right
D. Fuel Filter Differential Pressure Warning System Check
SUBTASK 73-00-00-200-001-F00
(1) Make sure that the malfunction is in the fuel filter bypass warning system.
(a) If the FILTER BYPASS light comes ON with the engine not in operation, continue to the
step to replace the fuel filter.
NOTE: The fuel filter bypass warning system is faulty.
1) To energize the Electronic Engine Control (EEC) with the engine not in operation,
put the Engine START Switch to the CONT position.
(b) If the FILTER BYPASS light does not come ON with the engine not in operation, do these
steps:
1) If the FILTER BYPASS light comes ON before engine shutdown, do this task: EEC
BITE TEST - RECENT FAULTS, TASK 73-21-00-740-803-F00.
a) Look for fault messages on Fuel Filter Signals Disagree.
<1> If you find the fault message, continue to the step to replace the fuel
filter.
2) If the CDS MAINT indication shows, do this task: CDS FAULT and/or CDS MAINT
Message Shows on the Display - Fault Isolation, FIM 31-62 TASK 884.
NOTE: The CDS MAINT message may indicate a Display Electronic Unit (DEU)
fault that affects the fuel filter bypass indication.
3) If the FILTER BYPASS light is inoperative OFF , do this task: EEC TEST ,
TASK 73-21-00-700-804-F00.
NOTE: The other indication lights in the EEC test must come on as usual.
SUBTASK 73-00-00-900-001-F00
(2) If it is necessary, replace the fuel filter (TASK 73-11-02-000-801-F00 and
TASK 73-11-02-400-801-F00).
NOTE: For Non-ER operations, one flight is permitted before filter replacement only if the fuel
drained from filter housing drain plug is free of visible gross contamination.

5. MMEL 73-12 (DDPG) Preparation - EEC Alternate Power Supply System Inoperative
A. General
(1) This task gives the maintenance steps which prepare the airplane for flight with the EEC
Alternate Power Supply System inoperative.
(2) Dispatch is not allowed with ENGINE CONTROL light(s) illuminated or inoperative.

73-11-01-600-801-F00Fuel Pump PreservationPDF p. 23

2. Fuel Pump Preservation
A. General
(1) Do this procedure when you send a fuel pump to storage.
B. References
Reference Title
73-11-01-000-801-F00 Fuel Pump Package Removal (P/B 401)
C. Tools/Equipment
NOTE: When more than one tool part number is listed under the same "Reference" number, the
tools shown are alternates to each other within the same airplane series. Tool part numbers
that are replaced or non-procurable are preceded by "Opt:", which stands for Optional.
Reference Description
SPL-2338 Adapter - Drive Spline, Fuel Pump
Part #: 856A3471P02 Supplier: 58828
D. Consumable Materials
Reference Description Specification
D00601 [CP2101] Vaseline - Graphite Mineral
D00623 [CP5066] Oil - Fuel System, Corrosion Preventive MIL-PRF-6081, Grade
1010
D00672 [CP5070] Vaseline - Pure Mineral V V-P-236
E. Fuel Pump Preservation
SUBTASK 73-11-01-620-001-F00
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB POISONOUS
MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
WARNING
DO NOT LET THE ENGINE FUEL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. FLUSH THE FUEL
FROM YOUR SKIN WITH WATER. THE FUEL IS POISONOUS AND CAN BE
ABSORBED INTO YOUR BODY .
(1) Do these steps to preserve the fuel pump with oil, D00623 [CP5066] or vaseline, D00672
[CP5070]:
NOTE: You can not do this procedure if the fuel pump is installed on the engine. Refer to this
task (TASK 73-11-01-000-801-F00) to remove the fuel pump.
(a) Make sure that the oil that you use for the procedure is clean.
NOTE: If it is necessary, put the oil through a 10 micron filter to clean it.
(b) Pour the oil into the inlet port of the pump.
73-11-01
(c) Use the adapter, SPL-2338 to turn the fuel pump main drive shaft until the oil flows out of
the discharge port.
(d) Tilt the pump and drain as much of the oil as possible.
(e) Lubricate the splines of the main and control drive shafts with graphite mineral vaseline,
D00601 [CP2101].
(f) Install the shipping closures from the replacement fuel pump.
73-11-01-000-801-F00Fuel Pump Package RemovalPDF p. 25
Open original AMM PDF - page 25 ↗ 
2. Fuel Pump Package Removal
(Figure 401, Figure 402, Figure 403, Figure 404, and Figure 405)
A. General
(1) This task gives the instructions to remove the fuel pump package.
(2) The fuel pump package is on the engine at the 8:00 o'clock position below the left fan cowl
panel.
(3) The fuel pump package is made up of the fuel pump, Hydro-Mechanical Unit (HMU), fuel filter,
main oil/fuel heat exchanger, servo fuel heater, and fuel filter differential pressure switch.
(4) It is easier to complete the task if the same size tube connectors are removed and installed at
the same time.
B. References
Reference Title
24-22-00-860-811 Supply Electrical Power (P/B 201)
24-22-00-860-812 Remove Electrical Power (P/B 201)

(P/B 201)

73-11-01-600-801-F00 Fuel Pump Preservation (P/B 201)

73-21-10-000-801-F00 HMU Removal (P/B 401)

Indicator Removal (P/B 401)
79-21-02-000-801-F00 Main Oil/Fuel Heat Exchanger Removal (P/B 401)

411 Engine 1 - Engine

G. Prepare for Removal
SUBTASK 73-11-01-760-002-F00
(1) Make sure that the electrical power is on (TASK 24-22-00-860-811).
SUBTASK 73-11-01-840-008-F00
(2) Do these steps to prepare the airplane for the removal:
(a) Make sure that the start levers are in the CUTOFF position.
1) Install the DO NOT OPERATE tags, STD-858, on the start levers.
(b) Make sure that the ENG VALVE CLOSED (engine fuel shutoff valve) light on the fuel
control panel (P5 overhead panel) is dim.
NOTE: The light for the engine fuel shutoff valve has three positions:
1) bright when the valve is in transition or does not agree with the commanded
position
2) dim when the valve is closed, or
3) off when the valve is opened.
(c) Make sure that the SPAR VALVE CLOSED (fuel spar shutoff valve) light on the fuel
control panel (P5 overhead panel) is dim.
(d) Do this task: Remove Electrical Power, TASK 24-22-00-860-812.
NOTE: The removal of the electrical power is necessary before the electrical and fluid
connectors are disconnected. Electrical power can be reapplied to the airplane
after all of the electrical and fluid connectors are disconnected and the protective
covers are installed.
(e) Make sure that the BAT switch, on the P5-13 panel, is set to the OFF position.
1) Install the DO NOT OPERATE tag, STD-858, on the BAT switch.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-010-004-F00
(3) Do this task: Open the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-010-801-F00.
H. Fuel Pump Package Removal
SUBTASK 73-11-01-680-002-F00
(1) Do these steps to drain the fuel from the fuel pump package [1].
73-11-01
WARNING
DO NOT GET FUEL IN YOUR MOUTH, OR EYES, OR ON YOUR SKIN.
DO NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. KEEP THE FUEL
AWAY FROM SPARKS, FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND
FLAMMABLE, WHICH CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS OR
DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
(a) Put a 5 gallon (19 liter) container, STD-1154, below the fuel pump.
(b) Remove the drain plug [2] from the fuel filter cover.
(c) Let the fuel drain in the container.
(d) Remove and discard the O-ring [3] from the drain plug [2].
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB
POISONOUS MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(e) Lubricate a new O-ring [3] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672 [CP5070].
(f) Install the new O-ring [3] on the drain plug [2].
(g) Lubricate the threads of the drain plug [2] with oil, D00623 [CP5066].
(h) Install the drain plug [2].
1) Tighten the drain plug [2] to 45 in-lb (5.1 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.2 N·m).
(i) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the drain
plug [2].
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-012-F00
(2) Do this task: Handcranking Drive Cover Removal, TASK 72-63-01-000-801-F00.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-013-F00
(3) Disconnect these electrical connectors:
NOTE: If it is necessary, use soft-nose pliers to turn the connector nuts.
(a) The DP1203 (MW0312) connector from the HMU
(b) The DP0501 (J5) connector from the HMU
(c) The DP1207 (MW0312) connector from the HMU
(d) The DP0601 (J6) connector from the HMU
(e) The DP0803 (J8) connector from the fuel filter differential pressure switch.
(f) Install protective covers on the plugs and receptacles.
1) Move the electrical connectors out of the way to make sure that they are not
damaged.
a) If it is necessary, use lockwire or tape to keep the connectors out of the way.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-210-002-F00
(4) Make sure that the container stays below the fuel pump during the removal procedure.
(a) Let the unwanted fuel and oil drain into the container while the fuel pump package [1] is
disassembled.
73-11-01
SUBTASK 73-11-01-420-004-F00
CAUTION
USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN THE TUBE COUPLING NUTS. USE ONE
TO HOLD THE MATING PART , AND THE OTHER TO LOOSEN THE
COUPLING NUT . IF YOU DO NOT USE TWO WRENCHES, DAMAGE TO THE
TUBES AND MATING PART CAN OCCUR.
(5) Disconnect these hoses from the HMU (TASK 73-21-10-000-801-F00):
(a) Use two wrenches to disconnect these hoses:
NOTE: The hose removals are arranged so that same wrenches are used at the same
time.
1) The HPT hose [5]
RYR ALL PRE SB CFM56-7B 73-044
2) The BSV hose [10]
NOTE: Engine POST CFMI SB 73-044 do not have the Burner Staging Valve (BSV)
hose installed.
RYR ALL
3) The TBV hose [12].
(b) Use two wrenches to disconnect these hoses:
1) The LPT hose [4]
2) The VSV hose (ROD) [6]
3) The VBV hose (CLOSED) [8]
4) The PCR hose [11].
(c) Use two wrenches to disconnect these hoses:
1) The VSV hose (HEAD) [7]
2) The VBV hose (OPEN) [9].
(d) Install protective covers on the hoses and the HMU.
1) If it is necessary, use lockwire or tape to keep the hoses out of the way.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-014-F00
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB POISONOUS
MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
CAUTION
USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN THE TUBE COUPLING NUTS. USE ONE
TO HOLD THE MATING PART , AND THE OTHER TO LOOSEN THE
COUPLING NUT . IF YOU DO NOT USE TWO WRENCHES, DAMAGE TO THE
TUBES AND MATING PART CAN OCCUR.
(6) Do these steps to remove the oil tube [16] from the fuel pump package [1]:
(a) Use two wrenches to disconnect the oil tube [16] from the oil tube [15].
(b) Remove the four bolts [18] that hold the oil tube [16] and gasket [17] to the servo fuel
heater.
(c) Remove the nut [23], bolt [24], and two clamps [22] that hold the tubes to the fuel pump
package [1].
73-11-01
(d) Remove the nut [25], bolt [26], and clamp [27] that hold the tube to the bracket.
(e) Remove the oil tube [16].
1) If the gasket [17] is serviceable, then keep it with the tube for the subsequent
installation (TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
(f) Install protective covers on the oil tube [16] and servo fuel heater.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-015-F00
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB POISONOUS
MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(7) Do these steps to disconnect the oil tube [20] from the fuel pump package [1]:
(a) Remove the four bolts [19] that hold the oil tube [20] and gasket [21] to the servo fuel
heater.
(b) Move the oil tube [20] out of the way, to make sure that it is not damaged.
1) If it is necessary, use lockwire or tape to keep the tube out of the way.
2) If the gasket [21] is serviceable, then keep it with the tube for the subsequent
installation (TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
(c) Install protective covers on the oil tube [20] and servo fuel heater.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-016-F00
CAUTION
USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN THE TUBE COUPLING NUTS. USE ONE
TO HOLD THE MATING PART , AND THE OTHER TO LOOSEN THE
COUPLING NUT . IF YOU DO NOT USE TWO WRENCHES, DAMAGE TO THE
TUBES AND MATING PART CAN OCCUR.
(8) Do these steps to remove the fuel tube [31] and fuel tube [37] from the fuel pump package [1]:
(a) Use two wrenches to disconnect the fuel tube [31] from the fuel tube [37].
(b) Remove the four bolts [30] that hold the fuel tube [31] and gasket [32] to the fuel pump
package [1].
(c) Remove the nut [40], bolt [41], and clamp [42] that hold the fuel tube [31] to the fuel pump
package [1].
(d) Remove the fuel tube [31].
1) If the gasket [32] is serviceable, then keep it with the tube for the subsequent
installation (TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
(e) Install protective covers on the fuel tube [31] and fuel pump package [1].
(f) Use two wrenches to disconnect the fuel tube [37] from the hose [33].
1) If it is necessary, use lockwire or tape to keep the hose [33] out of the way.
(g) Remove the four bolts [39] that hold the fuel tube [37] and gasket [38] to the fuel pump
package [1].
(h) Remove the nut [35] and clamp [34] that hold the fuel tube [37] to the fuel pump
package [1].
(i) Remove the fuel tube [37].
1) If the gasket [38] is serviceable, then keep it with the tube for the subsequent
installation (TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
73-11-01
(j) Hand tighten the nut [35] on the bolt [36].
(k) Install protective covers on the fuel tube [37], hose [33], and fuel pump package [1].
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-017-F00
CAUTION
USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN THE TUBE COUPLING NUTS. USE ONE
TO HOLD THE MATING PART , AND THE OTHER TO LOOSEN THE
COUPLING NUT . IF YOU DO NOT USE TWO WRENCHES, DAMAGE TO THE
TUBES AND MATING PART CAN OCCUR.
(9) Do these steps to remove the fuel tube [45] and fuel tube [46] from the fuel pump package [1]:
(a) Use two wrenches to disconnect the fuel tube [45] from the servo fuel heater and fuel
pump.
(b) Remove the fuel tube [45].
1) Install protective covers on the fuel tube [45], servo fuel heater, and fuel pump.
(c) Use two wrenches to disconnect the fuel tube [46] from the servo fuel heater.
(d) Remove the four bolts [48] that hold the fuel tube [46] and gasket [47] to the fuel pump
package [1].
(e) Remove the fuel tube [46].
1) If the gasket [47] is serviceable, then keep it with the tube for the subsequent
installation (TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
2) Install protective covers on the fuel tube [46], servo fuel heater, and fuel pump
package [1].

SUBTASK 73-11-01-410-003-F00
(16) Do these steps to remove the fuel pump package [1]:
(a) Make sure that the protective covers are installed on all of the openings in the fuel pump
package [1].
(b) Do these steps to install the fuel pump support equipment:
1) Install the cradle, SPL-2336, below the fuel pump package [1].
2) Lift the cradle, SPL-2336, with a hoist until it seats tightly below the fuel pump.
a) If the cradle, SPL-2336, does not seat tightly below the fuel pump, move the
handle on the fuel filter cover out of the way.
3) Adjust the sling to keep the clearance between the sling and the bottom of the main
oil/fuel heat exchanger flange.
4) Connect the fuel pump to the cradle, SPL-2336, with the strap that comes with the
tool.
73-11-01
(c) Remove the Quick Attach/Detach (QAD) bolt [81] and retaining packing [88].
1) Install it in the opposite position with the jacking block, SPL-2189.
a) Keep the ball seat washer [82] for the subsequent installation.
WARNING
BE CAREFUL WHEN YOU REMOVE AND INSTALL THE FUEL PUMP
PACKAGE. THE FUEL PUMP PACKAGE IS HEAVY . INJURY TO
PERSONS OR DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR.
CAUTION
DO NOT LIFT THE FUEL PUMP BY THE DRIVE SHAFT . DO NOT LET THE
DRIVE SHAFT SUPPORT THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP . IF THE
DRIVE SHAFT SUPPORTS THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP , IT CAN
CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE FUEL PUMP SEALS.
CAUTION
YOU MUST HOLD THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP AS YOU LOOSEN
THE QAD RING. IF YOU DO NOT CORRECTLY HOLD THE WEIGHT (ALL
OF THE TIME) AS YOU LOOSEN THE QAD RING, THE QAD RING WILL
NOT OPEN.
(d) Operate the hoist until it holds the weight of the fuel pump package [1].
NOTE: The fuel pump package weighs approximately 106 lb (48 kg).
(e) Do these steps to loosen the QAD ring (Figure 404).
1) Use a non-metallic hammer to hit the QAD ring [83] in the counterclockwise
direction.
CAUTION
DO NOT TIGHTEN THE QAD BOLT TO MORE THAN 300 POUND-
INCHES (34 NEWTON METERS). IF YOU TIGHTEN THE QAD BOLT
TOO MUCH, YOU CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE QAD RING.
2) Tighten the QAD bolt [81] to 300 in-lb (34 N·m).
3) Use a non-metallic hammer to hit the QAD ring [83] in the counterclockwise
direction.
4) Tighten the QAD bolt [81] to 300 in-lb (34 N·m).
5) Use a non-metallic hammer to hit the QAD ring [83] in the counterclockwise
direction.
6) Continue to hit the QAD ring [83] and tighten the bolt [81] until the bolt [81] moves to
the open position.
(f) Carefully disengage the fuel pump package [1] drive shaft from the Accessory Gear Box
(AGB).
(g) Remove the fuel pump package [1] from the engine.
1) Install covers on the AGB mounting face.
(h) Remove and discard the gasket [85] from the QAD ring.
(i) Remove and discard the O-ring [86] from the fuel pump drive shaft.
(j) Remove the fuel pump package [1] from the cradle, SPL-2336, and hoist.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-024-F00
(17) Do these steps to remove the fuel pump inlet tube [63] from the fuel pump package [1].
73-11-01
(a) Remove the four bolts [65] that hold the fuel pump inlet tube [63] and gasket [64] to the
fuel pump package [1].
(b) Remove the fuel pump inlet tube [63] and gasket [64].
1) If the gasket [64] is serviceable, then keep it with the tube for the subsequent
installation (TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
2) Install protective covers on the fuel pump inlet tube [63] and fuel pump package [1].
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-011-F00
(18) Do these steps if the fuel pump is replaced:
(a) Remove the Line Replaceable Unit (LRU)s from the fuel pump to install a replacement
fuel pump.
(b) Do this task: HMU Removal, TASK 73-21-10-000-801-F00.
(c) Do this task: Servo Fuel Heater Removal, TASK 73-11-07-000-801-F00.
(d) Do this task: Main Oil/Fuel Heat Exchanger Removal, TASK 79-21-02-000-801-F00.
(e) Do this task: Fuel Filter Differential Pressure Switch or Fuel Filter Clogging Indicator
Removal, TASK 73-34-01-000-801-F00.
SUBTASK 73-11-01-620-002-F00
(19) If it is necessary to preserve the fuel pump, do this task: Fuel Pump Preservation,
TASK 73-11-01-600-801-F00.

G. Prepare for the Installation
SUBTASK 73-11-01-840-006-F00
(1) Do these steps to install the parts from the defective fuel pump:
(a) Remove the protective covers from the disconnected parts.
(b) Do these steps to examine the parts:
1) Clean the mating surfaces of the parts and adjacent areas.
2) Examine the mating surfaces of the parts and adjacent areas.
(c) Install the protective covers on the disconnected parts.
1) Examine the gaskets to make sure that they are serviceable
(TASK 70-30-01-910-802-F00).
(d) Make sure that the drain plug [2] is correctly installed.
1) If it is not installed or is loose, install the drain plug [2].
a) Lubricate a new O-ring [3] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672
[CP5070].
b) Install the O-ring [3] on the drain plug [2].
c) Lubricate the threads of the drain plug [2] with oil, D00623 [CP5066].
d) Install the drain plug [2].
e) Tighten the drain plug [2] to 45.0 in-lb (5.1 N·m) - 55.0 in-lb (6.2 N·m).
f) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on
the drain plug [2].

H. Fuel Pump Package Installation
SUBTASK 73-11-01-480-001-F00
(1) Do these steps to prepare for the installation of the fuel pump package [1]:
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB
POISONOUS MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(a) Lubricate a new O-ring [86] with vaseline, D00672 [CP5070].
(b) Install the O-ring [86] in the groove of the fuel pump drive shaft.
(c) Lubricate a new Quick Attach/Detach (QAD) gasket [85] with vaseline, D00672 [CP5070].
(d) Install the QAD gasket [85] on the fixed part of the QAD ring [83].
(e) Lubricate the threads of the QAD bolt [81] with oil, D00599 [CP2442].
WARNING
BE CAREFUL WHEN YOU REMOVE AND INSTALL THE FUEL PUMP
PACKAGE. THE FUEL PUMP PACKAGE IS HEAVY . INJURY TO
PERSONS OR DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR.
CAUTION
DO NOT LIFT THE FUEL PUMP BY THE DRIVE SHAFT . DO NOT LET THE
DRIVE SHAFT SUPPORT THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP . IF THE
DRIVE SHAFT SUPPORTS THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP , IT CAN
CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE FUEL PUMP SEALS.
(f) Use the strap to install the fuel pump package [1] on the cradle, SPL-2336.
NOTE: The fuel pump package weighs approximately 106 lb (48 kg).
(g) Attach the cradle, SPL-2336, to a suitable hoist.
(h) Move the cradle, SPL-2336, into its correct position below the fuel pump mounting pad.
(i) Operate the hoist to align the fuel pump package [1] with the face of the Accessory Gear
Box (AGB).

SUBTASK 73-11-01-420-005-F00
(3) Do these steps to install the fuel pump package [1]:
(a) If it is necessary, turn the AGB output shaft to align with the fuel pump drive shaft
(TASK 72-63-01-000-801-F00).
1) Turn the handcranking drive pad with a 3/4-inch square drive to align the fuel pump
drive shaft splines with the AGB drive recess.
CAUTION
DO NOT LIFT THE FUEL PUMP BY THE DRIVE SHAFT . DO NOT LET THE
DRIVE SHAFT SUPPORT THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP . IF THE
DRIVE SHAFT SUPPORTS THE WEIGHT OF THE FUEL PUMP , IT CAN
CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE FUEL PUMP SEALS.
(b) Engage the fuel pump package [1] in the AGB.
(c) Turn the QAD ring [83] clockwise until it engages with the fuel pump flange.
(d) Make sure that the ball seat washer [82] is correctly installed on the QAD ring [83].
(e) Install the QAD bolt [81] and retaining packing [88] in the QAD ring [83].
CAUTION
DO NOT TIGHTEN THE QAD BOLT TO MORE THAN 327 IN-LB (37 N.M).
IF YOU TIGHTEN THE QAD BOLT TOO MUCH, YOU CAN CAUSE
DAMAGE TO THE QAD RING.
(f) Tighten the QAD bolt [81] to 195 in-lb (22 N·m) - 230 in-lb (26 N·m).
1) Lightly hit around the outer surface of the QAD ring [83] with a non-metallic hammer
or rubber mallet.
2) Loosen the QAD bolt [81].
(g) Tighten the QAD bolt [81] to 292 in-lb (33 N·m) - 327 in-lb (37 N·m).
1) Lightly hit all around the outer surface of the QAD ring [83] with a non-metallic
hammer or rubber mallet.
(h) Tighten the QAD bolt [81] 292 in-lb (33 N·m) - 327 in-lb (37 N·m).
(i) Measure the clearance between the QAD ring [83] and flange of the QAD ring [83]
support.
1) If the clearance is more than 1 in. (25.4 mm) or less than 0.25 in. (6.4 mm), replace
the QAD ring [83].
(j) Install the safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the QAD
bolt [81].
NOTE: The final torque of the QAD bolt on the QAD ring will be done at the end of this
procedure after the engine test.
(k) Attach to the QAD bolt [81] an indicator that mentions the need to finalize the tightening
procedure after the engine tests.
(l) Remove the hoist and cradle, SPL-2336, from the fuel pump package [1].
(m) Do this task: Handcranking Drive Cover Installation, TASK 72-63-01-400-801-F00.

The second bolt is at the diagonal opposite location of the first bolt.
The third bolt is at the bottom left side of the port.
The fourth bolt is at the diagonal opposite location of the third bolt.
1) Tighten the bolts [39] to 40 in-lb (4.5 N·m) - 45 in-lb (5.1 N·m).

2) Use two wrenches to tighten the coupling nut between the fuel tube [31] and fuel
tube [37] to 900 in-lb (101.7 N·m) - 1100 in-lb (124.3 N·m).
(q) Tighten the nut [35] and nut [40] that hold the clamp [80], clamp [34], and clamp [42] to
62 in-lb (7.0 N·m) - 71 in-lb (8.0 N·m).

SUBTASK 73-11-01-420-017-F00
CAUTION
DO NOT TIGHTEN THE QAD BOLT TO MORE THAN 327 IN-LB (37 N.M). IF

THE QAD RING.

(d) Tighten the QAD bolt [81] to 195 in-lb (22 N·m) - 230 in-lb (26 N·m).
(e) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the QAD
bolt [81].

H. Visual Inspection of the Impeller Rotation
SUBTASK 73-11-01-020-002-F00
(1) Do these steps to examine the impeller:
(a) Look through the opening where the lubrication flow screen (1) was removed to examine
the impeller.
(b) Examine the impeller as you turn the hand cranking drive with a 3/4-inch square drive.
(c) Make sure that the fuel pump impeller turns freely.
(d) Make sure that the surface of the impeller is free of visible damage.
1) Visible damage is not acceptable.
(e) If the impeller is not in the limits, replace the fuel pump.
1) These are the tasks:

1) Tighten the lubrication flow screen [1] to 90-110 pound-inches (10.1-12.4 Newton
meters).

2. Fuel Filter - Removal
(Figure 401, Figure 402, and Figure 403)
NOTE: This procedure is a scheduled maintenance task.
A. General
(1) Each engine has one fuel filter element on the fuel pump assembly.
B. References
Reference Title
24-22-00-860-812 Remove Electrical Power (P/B 201)
71-11-02-010-801-F00 Open the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201)

and Gear Housing Assembly (P/B 801)
C. Tools/Equipment
NOTE: When more than one tool part number is listed under the same "Reference" number, the
tools shown are alternates to each other within the same airplane series. Tool part numbers
that are replaced or non-procurable are preceded by "Opt:", which stands for Optional.
Reference Description
SPL-25294 Slide Hammer - Fuel Filter Cover, AGB
Part #: 856A8301G01 Supplier: 1Z812
Opt Part #: 856A8301G02 Supplier: 1Z812

421 Engine 2 - Engine
E. Prepare for the Removal
SUBTASK 73-11-02-840-001-F00
(1) Do these steps to prepare for the removal:
(a) Make sure that the engine start lever is in the CUTOFF position.
1) Install a DO NOT OPERATE tag, STD-858, on the applicable engine start lever.
(b) Make sure that the ENG VALVE CLOSED (engine fuel shutoff valve) light, on the P5
overhead panel (fuel control panel), is dim.
NOTE: The light for the engine fuel shutoff valve has three positions: Bright when the
valve is in transition or does not agree with the commanded position; Dim when
the valve is closed and off when the valve is opened.
73-11-02
(c) Make sure that the SPAR VALVE CLOSED light, on the P5 overhead panel (fuel control
panel), is dim.
NOTE: The light for the fuel spar valve has three positions: Bright when the valve is in
transition or does not agree with the commanded position; Dim when the valve is
closed and off when the valve is opened.
(d) Do this task: Remove Electrical Power, TASK 24-22-00-860-812.
NOTE: The removal of the electrical power is necessary while you disconnect the
electrical and fluid connectors. You can reapply electrical power to the airplane
after all of the electrical and fluid connectors are disconnected and the protective
covers are installed.
1) Make sure that the BAT switch, on the P5-13 panel, is set to the OFF position.
a) Install a DO NOT OPERATE tag, STD-858, on the BAT switch.
(e) Do this task: Open the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-010-801-F00.
F . Fuel Filter Removal
NOTE: There are different configurations of the fuel filter cover.
NOTE: The original design of the fuel pump filter cover attachment (bolts and fuel pump housing
inserts) was canceled. It must be reworked by SB CFM56-7B-73-A0034.
NOTE: The slide hammer, SPL-25294, can be used to remove the fuel filter cover from the main
fuel pump.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
SUBTASK 73-11-02-020-001-F00
(1) Do these steps to remove the fuel filter cover [6]:
NOTE: This Subtask is for engines that have a fuel filter cover attachment with five D-Head
bolts, five retaining rings, five washers, five nuts and one bolt with its insert (1 location)
into the fuel pump housing.
WARNING
DO NOT GET FUEL IN YOUR MOUTH, EYES, OR ON YOUR SKIN. DO
NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. PUT ON GOGGLES, AND
GLOVES WHEN YOU USE FUEL. KEEP FUEL AWAY FROM SPARKS,
FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND FLAMMABLE. FUEL CAN
CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
(a) Do these steps to drain the fuel system:
1) Put the 5 gallon (19 liter) container, STD-1154, under the fuel pump assembly.
2) Cut and remove the safety wire or cable from the drain plug [4].
3) Remove the drain plug [4] from the fuel filter cover [6].
a) Let the fuel drain in the container.
4) Remove and discard the packing [5] from the drain plug [4].
a) Keep the drain plug [4] for the installation.
(b) Remove the MW0312 wire harness from the Omega clip that is just to the left of the fuel
filter cover [6].
(c) Do these steps to remove the fuel filter cover [6]:
1) Loosen and remove the bolt [2] and flat washer [3] that hold the fuel filter cover [6].
a) Do an inspection of the bolt [2] for signs of thread damage.
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
b) Do an inspection of the flat washer [3] for signs of damage (nicks, flatness
condition).
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
2) Loosen and remove the five nuts [15] and five flat washers [14] that hold the fuel
filter cover [6].
NOTE: The five D-Head bolts are captive in the fuel filter cover housing.
a) Do an inspection of the nuts [15] for signs of thread damage.
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace them.
b) Do an inspection of the flat washers [14] for signs of damage (nicks, flatness
condition).
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace them.
3) Remove the fuel filter cover [6] from the fuel filter housing.
NOTE: When you remove the fuel filter cover, the filter element remains attached to
it.
a) Do an inspection of the main fuel pump housing insert for signs of damage for
a pulled out insert movement, and the thread insert damage.
NOTE: There is only one insert in the main fuel pump housing, located in the
hole adjacent to the nameplate.
<1> If the insert is damaged, replace the fuel pump or apply SB
CFM56-7B-73-0079.
b) Do an inspection of the attached parts as follows:
<1> Do an inspection of the five D-Head bolts [12] and five retaining
rings [13] for signs of damage.
<a> If there is damage, discard and replace the D-Head bolts [12]
and retaining rings [13].
c) Do an inspection of the mating surfaces of the fuel filter cover and fuel filter
housing (Figure 403).
<1> Do an inspection of the fuel filter cover for nicks and dents.
<a> If nicks and dents are within the repairable limits, repair the part
(TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
<2> Do an inspection of the fuel filter cover for scratches and scores.
<a> If scratches and scores are within the repairable limits, repair the
part (TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
<3> Do an inspection of the fuel filter housing for nicks and dents.
<a> If nicks and dents are within the repairable limits, repair the part
(TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
<4> Do an inspection on the fuel filter housing for scratches and scores.
<a> If scratches and scores are within the repairable limits, repair the
part (TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
4) Remove and discard the packing [7] from the fuel filter cover [6].
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
5) Remove the fuel filter element [11] from the fuel filter cover [6].
a) Do the inspection of the fuel filter cover [6] for contamination.
b) Do the inspection the fuel filter element [11] for contamination.
<1> If you find usual contamination, do the following step:
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
<a> Discard the fuel filter element [11], packing [1], and packing [10]
that are attached to the filter element [11].
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
<b> Discard the fuel filter element [11] and packings that are attached
to the filter element [11].
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
<c> Make sure that the packings are attached to the fuel filter
element and not stuck in the filter housing.
c) If you find large quantities of contamination, do the following step:
<1> Do this task: FIM 73-05 TASK 801.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
SUBTASK 73-11-02-020-002-F00
(2) Do these steps to remove the fuel filter cover [6]:
NOTE: This Subtask is for engines that have a fuel filter cover attachment with five D-Head
bolts, five retaining rings, five flat washers, five nuts and one bolt with one washer, one
self-aligning washer and one self-aligning nut.
WARNING
DO NOT GET FUEL IN YOUR MOUTH, EYES, OR ON YOUR SKIN. DO
NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. PUT ON GOGGLES, AND
GLOVES WHEN YOU USE FUEL. KEEP FUEL AWAY FROM SPARKS,
FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND FLAMMABLE. FUEL CAN
CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
(a) Do these steps to drain the fuel system:
1) Put the 5 gallon (19 liter) container, STD-1154, under the fuel pump assembly.
2) Cut and remove the safety wire or cable from the drain plug [4].
3) Remove the drain plug [4] from the fuel filter cover [6].

(c) Do these steps to remove the fuel filter cover [6]:
1) Loosen and remove the five nuts [15] and five flat washers [14] that hold the fuel

NOTE: If there is damage, discard and replace them.
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
a) Do an inspection of the nuts [15] for signs of thread damage.
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
b) Do an inspection of the flat washers [14] for signs of damage (nicks, flatness
condition).
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
2) Loosen and remove the bolt [16], flat washers [17], self-aligning washer [18], and
self-aligning nut [19] that hold the fuel filter cover [6].
a) Do an inspection of bolt [16] for signs of thread damage.
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
b) Do an inspection of the flat washers [17] for signs of damage (nicks, flatness
condition).
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
c) Do an inspection of the self-aligning washer [18] for signs of thread and
spherical face damage.
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
d) Do an inspection of the self-aligning nut [19] for signs of thread and spherical
face damage.
<1> If there is damage, discard and replace it.
3) Remove the fuel filter cover [6] from the fuel filter housing.
NOTE: When you remove the fuel filter cover, the fuel filter element remains
attached to it.
a) Do an inspection of the attached parts as follows:
<1> Do an inspection of the five D-Head bolts [12] and five retaining
rings [13] for signs of damage.
<a> If there is damage, discard and replace the D-Head bolts [12]
and retaining rings [13].
b) Do an inspection of the mating surfaces of the fuel filter cover and fuel filter
housing (Figure 403).
<1> Do an inspection of the fuel filter cover for nicks and dents.
<a> If nicks and dents are within the repairable limits, repair the part
(TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
<2> Do an inspection of the fuel filter cover for scratches and scores.
<a> If scratches and scores are within the repairable limits, repair the
part (TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
<3> Do an inspection of the fuel filter housing for nicks and dents.
<a> If nicks and dents are within the repairable limits, repair the part
(TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
<4> Do an inspection on the fuel filter housing for scratches and scores.
<a> If scratches and scores are within the repairable limits, repair the
part (TASK 73-11-11-300-801-F00).
4) Remove and discard the packing [7] from the fuel filter cover [6].
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
5) Remove the fuel filter element [11] from the fuel filter cover [6].
6) Do an inspection of the fuel filter cover [6] for contamination.
7) Do an inspection of the fuel filter element [11] for contamination.
a) If you find usual contamination, do the following step:
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
<1> Discard the fuel filter element [11], packing [1], and packing [10].
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
<2> Discard the fuel filter element [11] and packings attached to it.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
<3> Make sure that the packings are attached to the fuel filter element and
not stuck in the fuel filter housing.
8) If you find large quantities of contamination, do the following step:
a) Do this task: FIM 73-05 TASK 801.
RYR ALL

E. Prepare for the Installation
SUBTASK 73-11-02-840-002-F00
(1) Do these steps to prepare for the installation:
WARNING
DO NOT USE THE SOLVENT NEAR AN OPEN FLAME. THE SOLVENT IS
FLAMMABLE. DO NOT BREATHE THE FUMES OF THE SOLVENT . IF
YOU DO NOT OBEY THESE INSTRUCTIONS, INJURIES TO
PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR.
WARNING
ISOPROPYL ALCOHOL IS TOXIC AND FLAMMABLE. USE PERSONAL
PROTECTION EQUIPMENT . USE IN A WELL-VENTILATED AREA.
(a) Use fuel, G02272, alcohol, B00676 [CP1041], or aviation turbine fuel, G51056, and
cotton wiper, G00034, to clean the fuel filter housing and fuel filter cover [6].
(b) Examine all mating surfaces and adjacent areas of the fuel filter housing to make sure
that they are clean and in a good condition.
F . Fuel Filter Installation
NOTE: There are different configurations of the fuel filter cover.
NOTE: The original design of the fuel pump filter cover attachment (bolts and fuel pump housing
inserts) was cancelled. It must be reworked by SB CFM56-7B-73-A0034.
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
SUBTASK 73-11-02-420-001-F00
(1) Do these steps to install the fuel filter:
NOTE: This Subtask is for engines that have a fuel filter cover attachment with five D-Head
bolts, five retaining rings, five washers, five nuts and one bolt with its insert (1 location)
into the fuel pump housing.
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB
POISONOUS MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(a) Install the new packing [7] in the fuel filter cover [6].
1) Lubricate the packing [7] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672 [CP5070].
2) Install the packing [7] in the groove of the fuel filter cover [6].
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB
POISONOUS MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(b) Lubricate and install the new packing [1] and new packing [10] on the new fuel filter
element [11].
NOTE: These steps are for fuel filter with packings not installed in their grooves.
1) Lubricate the packing [1] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672 [CP5070].
2) Install the packing [1] in the groove at the top of the fuel filter element [11].
3) Lubricate the packing [10] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672
[CP5070].
4) Install the packing [10] in the groove at the bottom of the fuel filter element [11].
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB
POISONOUS MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(c) Lubricate the packings of the new fuel filter element [11] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or
vaseline, D00672 [CP5070].
NOTE: The packings are already installed by the manufacturer in their grooves on the
new fuel filter element.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
(d) Lightly lubricate the threads and mating face of the bolt [2] with graphite mineral vaseline,
D00601 [CP2101].
(e) Lightly lubricate the threads of the five D-Head bolts [12] and mating faces of the five
nuts [15] with graphite mineral vaseline, D00601 [CP2101].
1) Make sure that the five retaining rings [13] are correctly installed.
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
CAUTION
MAKE SURE THAT YOU CORRECTLY INSTALL THE WAVE SPRING AND
THE RETAINING RING ON THE FUEL FILTER COVER. IF YOU DO NOT
INSTALL CORRECTLY , DAMAGE TO THE FUEL FILTER ELEMENT CAN
OCCUR.
(f) Make sure that the wave spring [8] and retaining ring [9] are correctly installed into the
fuel filter cover [6].
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
(g) Carefully install the fuel filter element [11] in the fuel filter cover [6].
1) Look down the center of the filter element [11] for signs of extrusion and/or cut
packing at the filter cover/packing interface.
2) Make sure that the packing [10] stays in its correct position in the groove in the fuel
filter element [11].
3) Make sure that the packing [7] stays in its correct position in the groove in the fuel
filter cover [6].
4) Make sure that the fuel filter element [11] does not move.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
(h) Carefully install the fuel filter element [11] in the fuel filter cover [6].
1) Look down the center of the filter element [11] for signs of extrusion and/or cut
packing at the filter cover/packing interface.
2) Adjust the tabs of the fuel filter element [11] to the fuel filter cover.
3) Make sure that the packings stay in their grooves in the fuel filter element [11].
4) Make sure that the packing [7] stays in its correct position in the groove in the fuel
filter cover [6].
5) Make sure that the fuel filter element [11] does not move.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
(i) Carefully install the fuel filter cover [6] and fuel filter element [11] into the fuel filter
housing.
1) Make sure that the fuel filter element [11] is correctly installed on the guide of the
fuel filter housing.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0078 AND PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
2) Adjust the tabs of the fuel filter element [11] between the ribs of the fuel filter
housing.
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079
CAUTION
DO NOT USE THE BOLTS TO HELP ENGAGE THE FILTER COVER.
DAMAGE ON THE INSERT WILL OCCUR.
3) Make sure that the fuel filter cover [6] is correctly engaged into the fuel filter
housing.
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
(j) Before the bolt installation, make sure that the fuel filter cover [6] is correctly installed
against the housing flange.
NOTE: A wood or plastic hammer may help to engage the cover, if it is necessary.
(k) Install the five flat washers [14] and five nuts [15] on the five D-Head bolts [12] that hold
the fuel filter cover [6] to the fuel filter housing.
1) Make sure that the nuts [15] screw freely by hand, do these steps:
NOTE: No tool is permitted.
a) Turn each nut for a minimum of two full turns.
<1> If the attached part is in a good condition, it should not be possible to
fully hand tighten the nut.
CAUTION
BE CAREFUL WHILE YOU TIGHTEN THE BOLTS THAT HOLD THE
FUEL FILTER COVER TO THE HOUSING. MAKE SURE THAT YOU
USE THE CORRECT TORQUE PROCEDURE WHEN YOU TIGHTEN
THE BOLTS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY INSTRUCTIONS, YOU CAN
CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE FUEL FILTER COVER OR CAUSE A FUEL
LEAK.
2) Tighten the five nuts [15] to 70 in-lb (7.9 N·m) - 80 in-lb (9.0 N·m)
(TASK 70-20-02-400-801-F00).
(l) Install the bolt [2] and flat washer [3] that hold the fuel filter cover [6] to the fuel filter
housing.
NOTE: Only one bolt is used in the hole adjacent to the nameplate.
1) Make sure that the bolt engages freely by hand, do these steps:
NOTE: No tool is permitted.
a) Hold the bolt head between your fingers, and turn the bolt for a minimum of
two full turns.
<1> If the insert is in a good condition, it should not be possible to fully hand
tighten the bolt.
<2> If the insert is not in a good condition, replace the fuel pump.
CAUTION
TIGHTEN THE BOLTS TO THE CORRECT TORQUE. THE
INCORRECT TORQUE CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE
COMPONENTS. LARGE QUANTITIES OF FUEL LEAKAGE WILL
OCCUR.
2) Tighten the bolt [2] to 70 in-lb (7.9 N·m) - 80 in-lb (9.0 N·m)
(TASK 70-20-02-400-801-F00).
(m) Install the MW0312 wire harness in the Omega clip that is immediately to the left of the
fuel filter cover [6].
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. YOU CAN ABSORB
POISONOUS MATERIALS FROM THE OIL THROUGH YOUR SKIN.
(n) Install the drain plug [4] on the fuel filter cover [6].
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES PRE SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
1) Lubricate a new packing [5] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672
[CP5070].
2) Install the packing [5] in the groove of the drain plug [4].
3) Lubricate the threads of the drain plug [4] with oil, D00623 [CP5066].
4) Install the drain plug [4] in the fuel filter cover [6].
a) Tighten the drain plug [4] to 45 in-lb (5.1 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.2 N·m).
5) Install the safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
drain plug [4].

1) Lubricate the head faces of the bolt [16] in contact with the flat washer [17].

2) Install the bolt [16], flat washer [17], self-aligning washer [18], and self-aligning
nut [19] for each assembly configuration.
NOTE: An improper bolt, nut and washer installation can lead to damages. Pay
special attention to the self-aligning nut and washer installation.
NOTE: Do not intermix the bolt, self-aligning nut and self-aligning washer from the
different suppliers on the fuel pump which attach the fuel filter cover.
73-11-02
RYR ALL; AIRPLANES POST SB-CFM-56-7B-73-0079 (Continued)
CAUTION
TIGHTEN THE BOLTS TO THE CORRECT TORQUE. THE
INCORRECT TORQUE CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE
COMPONENTS. LARGE QUANTITIES OF FUEL LEAKAGE WILL
OCCUR.
3) Tighten the bolt [16] and self-aligning nut [19] to 70 in-lb (7.9 N·m) - 80 in-lb
(9.0 N·m) (TASK 70-20-02-400-801-F00).
(m) Install the MW0312 wire harness in Omega clip that is immediately to the left of the fuel
filter cover [6].
WARNING
DO NOT LET OIL STAY ON YOUR SKIN. THE SYNTHETIC OIL
CONTAINS ADDITIVES THAT CAN BE POISONOUS IF THEY ARE
ABSORBED THROUGH THE SKIN. CLEAN AWAY ALL OIL THAT
GETS ON THE SKIN.
1) Install the drain plug [4] on the fuel filter cover [6].
a) Lubricate the new packing [5] with oil, D00623 [CP5066], or vaseline, D00672
[CP5070].
b) Install the packing [5] in the groove of the drain plug [4].
c) Lubricate the threads of the drain plug [4] with oil, D00623 [CP5066].
d) Install the drain plug [4] in the fuel filter cover [6].
<1> Tighten the drain plug [4] to 45 in-lb (5.1 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.2 N·m).
e) Install the safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006],
on the drain plug [4].

E. Fuel Nozzle Filter Removal (SAC)
SUBTASK 73-11-03-020-002-F00
(1) Remove the fuel outlet tube [5]:
(a) Remove the nut [6], bolt [8], and clamp [7] that hold the fuel outlet tube [5] to the bracket.
WARNING
DO NOT GET FUEL IN YOUR MOUTH, OR EYES, OR ON YOUR SKIN.
DO NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. KEEP THE FUEL
AWAY FROM SPARKS, FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND
FLAMMABLE, WHICH CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS OR
DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
(b) Put a 5-gallon (19-liter) fuel resistant container, STD-1054, below the connection between
the fuel outlet tube [5] and 9:00 O'clock strut tube.
CAUTION

ONE TO HOLD THE MATING PART , AND THE OTHER TO LOOSEN THE
COUPLING NUT . IF YOU DO NOT USE TWO WRENCHES, DAMAGE TO
THE TUBES AND MATING PART CAN OCCUR.
(c) Use two wrenches to disconnect the fuel outlet tube [5] from the 9:00 O'clock strut tube.
1) Let the unwanted fuel flow into the container.

1) Remove the fuel outlet tube [5].
(e) Install protective covers on the fuel outlet tube [5], 9:00 O'clock strut tube, and fuel nozzle
filter [4].

(b) Remove the two bolts [3] and washers [2] that hold the fuel nozzle filter [4] to the
brackets.
1) Remove the fuel nozzle filter [4].
(c) Install protective covers on the fuel inlet tube [1] and fuel nozzle filter [4].

2. Fuel Nozzle Removal
A. General
(1) The engine has twenty fuel nozzles.
(a) To remove the fuel nozzle from positions 1, 2, and 20, it is necessary to partially remove
the bleed air precooler.
(b) To remove the nozzle from position 7, you must remove the LPT duct.

WARNING

NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. KEEP THE FUEL AWAY FROM
SPARKS, FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND FLAMMABLE,
WHICH CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS OR DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
(1) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle [24] from position 1 (Figure 401, Figure 402,
Figure 403):
(a) Do this task: Bleed Air Precooler Disconnection (For Engine Component Removal),
TASK 36-12-01-800-801.
(b) Do the applicable steps in the T3 Sensor removal task to disconnect the T3 line from the
combustion case and from the support bracket (TASK 73-21-07-000-801-F00).
NOTE: Access to the fuel nozzle is easier when the T3 line is disconnected from the
combustion case and the support bracket.
1) Carefully move the T3 line out of the way.
(c) Disconnect the shroud [27] from the fuel nozzle [24].
1) Move the shroud [27] to get access to the coupling nut [25] on the fuel manifold [21].
(d) Disconnect the coupling nut [25] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(e) Remove the bolt [44] that holds the loop clamp [43] to the support bracket [42].
(f) Remove the bolts [23] and bolts [41] from the fuel nozzle flange.
1) Remove the support bracket [42].
(g) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle from the combustion case:
1) Move the fuel nozzle forward to disengage the fuel nozzle tip from the combustion
chamber.
2) Remove the fuel nozzle.
NOTE: You must turn the fuel nozzle clockwise as you remove it from the engine.
The tip of the nozzle will point forward as it comes out of the engine.
73-11-04
Config 1
(h) Remove the seal [28] and retainer [29] assembly from the fuel nozzle port on the
combustion case.
1) Discard the seal [28].
(i) Remove and discard the preformed packing [22] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(j) Remove and discard the preformed packing [26] from the fuel manifold [21].
(k) Visually examine the fuel nozzle wear sleeves.
1) If the wear sleeves are dislodged, replace the fuel nozzles and do this step:
NOTE: Fuel nozzles with wear sleeves that are dislodged are not serviceable.
a) Do a borescope inspection of the combustion chamber for downstream
damage (TASK 72-00-00-200-805-F00).
<1> If you cannot do the borescope inspection, continue-in-service for five
cycles.
2) If the wear sleeves have four interrupted weld design, replace the fuel nozzle:
NOTE: Fuel nozzles that have four interrupted weld design are not serviceable.
a) If you do not replace the fuel nozzle, continue-in-service for five cycles.
(l) Install protective covers on the fuel nozzle, the fuel nozzle port, and the fuel manifold
connection.

1) Remove the two bolts on the EGT harness and carefully move the EGT harness out
of the way.
2) Make sure that you install protective covers on the 9th stage inlet tube and the
HPTACC valve.
(b) Disconnect the shroud [27] from the fuel nozzle [24].
1) Move the shroud [27] to get access to the coupling nut [25] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Disconnect the coupling nut [25] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(d) Remove the bolts [23] and bolts [41] from the flange of the fuel nozzle.
(e) Remove the nut [104] and bolt [102] that hold the loop clamp [101] to the bracket [103].
1) Remove the bracket [103].
(f) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle from the combustion case:
1) Move the fuel nozzle forward to disengage the fuel nozzle tip from the combustion
chamber.
2) Remove the fuel nozzle.
NOTE: You must turn the fuel nozzle clockwise as you remove it from the engine.
The tip of the nozzle will point forward as it comes out of the engine.
73-11-04
Config 1
(g) Remove the seal [28] and retainer [29] assembly from the fuel nozzle port on the
combustion case.
1) Discard the seal [28].
(h) Remove and discard the preformed packing [22] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(i) Remove and discard the preformed packing [26] from the fuel manifold [21].
(j) Visually examine the fuel nozzle wear sleeves.
1) If the wear sleeves are dislodged, replace the fuel nozzles and do this step:
NOTE: Fuel nozzles with wear sleeves that are dislodged are not serviceable.
a) Do a borescope inspection of the combustion chamber for downstream
damage (TASK 72-00-00-200-805-F00).
<1> If you cannot do the borescope inspection, continue-in-service for five
cycles.
2) If the wear sleeves have four interrupted weld design, replace the fuel nozzle:
NOTE: Fuel nozzles that have four interrupted weld design are not serviceable.
a) If you do not replace the fuel nozzle, continue-in-service for five cycles.

DO NOT GET FUEL IN YOUR MOUTH, OR EYES, OR ON YOUR SKIN. DO
NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. KEEP THE FUEL AWAY FROM
SPARKS, FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND FLAMMABLE,
WHICH CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS OR DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT .
(17) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle from position 17 (Figure 401, Figure 402,
Figure 412):
(a) Disconnect the shroud [27] from the fuel nozzle [24].
1) Move the shroud [27] to get access to the coupling nut [25] on the fuel manifold [21].
(b) Disconnect the coupling nut [25] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(c) Remove the bolts [23] from the flange of the fuel nozzle.
(d) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle from the combustion case:
1) Move the fuel nozzle forward to disengage the fuel nozzle tip from the combustion
chamber.
2) Remove the fuel nozzle.
NOTE: You must turn the fuel nozzle clockwise as you remove it from the engine.
The tip of the nozzle will point forward as it comes out of the engine.
(e) Remove the seal [28] and retainer [29] assembly from the fuel nozzle port on the
combustion case.
1) Discard the seal [28].
(f) Remove and discard the preformed packing [22] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(g) Remove and discard the preformed packing [26] from the fuel manifold [21].
(h) Visually examine the fuel nozzle wear sleeves.
1) If the wear sleeves are dislodged, replace the fuel nozzles and do this step:
NOTE: Fuel nozzles with wear sleeves that are dislodged are not serviceable.
a) Do a borescope inspection of the combustion chamber for downstream
damage (TASK 72-00-00-200-805-F00).
<1> If you cannot do the borescope inspection, continue-in-service for five
cycles.
2) If the wear sleeves have four interrupted weld design, replace the fuel nozzle:
NOTE: Fuel nozzles that have four interrupted weld design are not serviceable.
a) If you do not replace the fuel nozzle, continue-in-service for five cycles.
(i) Install protective covers on the fuel nozzle, fuel nozzle port, and fuel manifold connection.

Config 1

E. Fuel Nozzle Installation
SUBTASK 73-11-04-420-049-F01
(1) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle at position 1 (Figure 401, Figure 402, Figure 403):
(a) Remove the protective cover from the fuel nozzle, fuel nozzle port, and fuel manifold
connection.
(b) Do these steps to install the new preformed packing [22] and preformed packing [26]:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Install the preformed packing [22] on the fuel nozzle [24].
3) Install the preformed packing [26] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Install a new seal [28] into the retainer [29].
(d) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle:
73-11-04
Config 1
1) Put the retainer [29] on the fuel nozzle port.
2) Put the fuel nozzle [24] into the fuel nozzle port, with the tip forward.
3) Turn the fuel nozzle counterclockwise while you move it on to the combustion case
pad.
4) Move the fuel nozzle aft to engage the tip in the combustion chamber.
(e) Apply graphite mineral vaseline, D00601 [CP2101], or Pure Nickel Special compound,
D00006, to the threads of the bolts [23] and bolt [41].
(f) Put the support bracket [42] in its correct position on the fuel nozzle flange.
(g) Install the bolts [23] and bolts [41] in the fuel nozzle flange.
1) Tighten the bolts [23] and bolts [41] to 110 in-lb (12.4 N·m) - 120 in-lb (13.6 N·m).
2) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
bolts.
(h) Install the bolt [44] that holds the loop clamp [43] to the support bracket [42].
1) Tighten the bolt [44] to 62 in-lb (7.0 N·m) - 68 in-lb (7.7 N·m).
(i) Do these steps to install the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21] to the fuel
nozzle [24]:
1) Connect the coupling nut [25] to the nipple on the fuel nozzle [24].
a) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
2) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
3) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
4) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
5) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
6) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
coupling nut [25].
(j) Do the applicable steps in the T3 Sensor Installation task to re-connect the T3 line to the
combustion case and to the support bracket (TASK 73-21-07-400-801-F00).
(k) Do this task: Bleed Air Precooler Reconnection (After Engine Component Installation),
TASK 36-12-01-400-801.
SUBTASK 73-11-04-420-050-F01
(2) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle at position 2 (Figure 401, Figure 402, Figure 404):
(a) Remove the protective cover from the fuel nozzle, fuel nozzle port, and fuel manifold
connection.

1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Install the preformed packing [22] on the fuel nozzle [24].
3) Install the preformed packing [26] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Install a new seal [28] into the retainer [29].
(d) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle:
1) Put the retainer [29] on the fuel nozzle port.
2) Put the fuel nozzle [24] into the fuel nozzle port, with the tip forward.

Config 1
3) Turn the fuel nozzle counterclockwise while you move it on to the combustion case
pad.
4) Move the fuel nozzle aft to engage the tip in the combustion chamber.

73-11-04
Config 1

3) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).

6) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
coupling nut [25].
(j) Reconnect the LPT cooling air tube to the combustion case and compressor case as
follows:
1) Make sure that the metal ring seal is in place.
2) Apply graphite mineral vaseline, D00601 [CP2101], to the bolts.
3) Tighten the bolts to 62 in-lb (7.0 N·m) - 68 in-lb (7.7 N·m) and safety with safety
wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety wire, G02495 [CP8002].

(a) Remove the protective cover from the fuel nozzle, fuel nozzle port, and fuel manifold
connection.
(b) Do these steps to install the new preformed packing [22] and preformed packing [26]:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070] on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Install the preformed packing [22] on the fuel nozzle [24].
3) Install the preformed packing [26] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Install a new seal [28] into the retainer [29].
(d) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle:
1) Put the retainer [29] on the fuel nozzle port.
2) Put the fuel nozzle [24] into the fuel nozzle port, with the tip forward.
3) Turn the fuel nozzle counterclockwise while you move it on to the combustion case
pad.
4) Move the fuel nozzle aft to engage the tip in the combustion chamber.
(e) Apply graphite mineral vaseline, D00601 [CP2101], or Pure Nickel Special compound,
D00006, to the threads of the bolts [23] and bolts [41].
(f) Move the hinged bracket [61] into its position on the fuel nozzle flange.
(g) Install the bolts [23] and bolts [41] in the fuel nozzle flange.
1) Tighten the bolts [23] and bolts [41] to 110 in-lb (12.4 N·m) - 120 in-lb (13.6 N·m).
2) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
bolts.
(h) Do these steps to install the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21] to the fuel
nozzle [24]:
1) Connect the coupling nut [25] to the nipple on the fuel nozzle [24].
a) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
2) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
3) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
4) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
5) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
6) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
coupling nut [25].
(i) Connect the PS3 line to the combustion case, and to the two PS3 brackets closest to the
combustion case.

2. Fuel Nozzle Removal
A. General
(1) The engine has twenty fuel nozzles.
(a) To remove the fuel nozzle from positions 1, 2, and 20, it is necessary to partially remove
the bleed air precooler.
(b) To remove the nozzle from position 7, you must remove the LPT duct.
B. References
Reference Title
24-22-00-860-811 Supply Electrical Power (P/B 201)
24-22-00-860-812 Remove Electrical Power (P/B 201)
36-12-01-800-801 Bleed Air Precooler Disconnection (For Engine Component
Removal) (P/B 201)
72-00-00-200-805-F00 Combustion Section Borescope Inspection (P/B 601)
75-22-04-000-802-F00 LPTACC Valve Removal (P/B 401)
78-31-00-010-801-F00 Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201)
C. Location Zones
Zone Area
411 Engine 1 - Engine
421 Engine 2 - Engine
D. Prepare for the Removal
SUBTASK 73-11-04-840-011-F02
(1) Make sure that the engine fuel valves are closed:
(a) Do this task: Supply Electrical Power, TASK 24-22-00-860-811.
(b) Make sure the start levers are in the CUTOFF position.
1) Install a DO-NOT-OPERATE tag on the applicable start lever.
(c) Make sure the ENG VALVE CLOSED (engine fuel shutoff valve) light on the fuel control
panel (P5 overhead panel) is dim.
NOTE: The light for the engine fuel shutoff valve has three positions: 1) bright when the
valve is in transition or does not agree with the commanded position; 2) dim
when the valve is closed; or 3) off when the valve is opened.
SUBTASK 73-11-04-840-012-F02
(2) Prepare the airplane for the procedure:
(a) Do this task: Remove Electrical Power, TASK 24-22-00-860-812.
73-11-04
Config 2
1) Make sure that the BAT switch on panel P5-13 is set to OFF and install a
DO-NOT-OPERATE tag.
WARNING
DO THESE SPECIFIED TASKS IN THE CORRECT SEQUENCE BEFORE
YOU OPEN THE THRUST REVERSERS: RETRACT THE LEADING EDGE,
DO THE DEACTIVATION PROCEDURES FOR THE LEADING EDGE AND
THE THRUST REVERSERS (FOR GROUND MAINTENANCE), AND OPEN
THE FAN COWL PANELS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY THE ABOVE
SEQUENCE, INJURIES TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT
CAN OCCUR.
(b) Do this task: Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-801-F00.
E. Fuel Nozzle Removal
SUBTASK 73-11-04-020-038-F02
WARNING
DO NOT GET FUEL IN YOUR MOUTH, OR EYES, OR ON YOUR SKIN. DO
NOT BREATHE THE FUMES FROM THE FUEL. KEEP THE FUEL AWAY FROM
SPARKS, FLAME, AND HEAT . FUEL IS POISONOUS AND FLAMMABLE,
WHICH CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO
EQUIPMENT .
(1) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle [24] from position 1 or 19 (Figure 401, Figure 402,
Figure 403):
(a) For fuel nozzle [24] in position 1, do this task: Bleed Air Precooler Disconnection (For
Engine Component Removal), TASK 36-12-01-800-801.
(b) Disconnect the shroud [27] from the fuel nozzle [24].
1) Move the shroud [27] to get access to the coupling nut [25] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Disconnect the coupling nut [25] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(d) Remove the bolt [44] that holds the loop clamp [43] to the support bracket [42].
(e) Remove the three bolts [23] and [41] from the fuel nozzle flange.
1) Remove the support bracket [42].
(f) Do these steps to remove the fuel nozzle from the combustion case:
1) Move the fuel nozzle forward to disengage the fuel nozzle tip from the combustion
chamber.
2) Remove the fuel nozzle.
NOTE: You must turn the fuel nozzle 180 degrees clockwise as you remove it from
the engine. The tip of the nozzle will point forward as it comes out of the
engine.
(g) Remove the seal [28] and retainer [29] assembly from the fuel nozzle port on the
combustion case.
1) Remove and discard the seal [28].
(h) Remove and discard the preformed packing [22] from the fuel nozzle [24].
(i) Remove and discard the preformed packing [26] from the fuel manifold [21].
(j) Visually examine the fuel nozzle wear sleeves.
1) If the wear sleeves are dislodged, they are not serviceable.
a) Replace the fuel nozzles.
73-11-04
Config 2
b) Use a borescope to examine the combustion chamber for downstream
damage (TASK 72-00-00-200-805-F00) or continue-in-service for five (5)
cycles.
2) If the wear sleeves have four (4) interrupted weld design, they are not serviceable.
a) Replace the fuel nozzles or continue-in-service for five (5) cycles.
(k) Install protective covers on the fuel nozzle, the fuel nozzle port, and the fuel manifold
connection.

1) Move the fuel nozzle forward to disengage the fuel nozzle tip from the combustion
chamber.
73-11-04
Config 2
2) Remove the fuel nozzle.
NOTE: You must turn the fuel nozzle 180 degrees clockwise as you remove it from
the engine. The tip of the nozzle will point forward as it comes out of the
engine.
(g) Remove the seal [28] and retainer [29] assembly from the fuel nozzle port on the
combustion case.
1) Remove and discard the seal [28].
(h) Remove the packing [22] from the fuel nozzle [24].
1) Discard the packing [22].
(i) Remove the packing [26] from the fuel manifold [21].
1) Discard the packing [26].
(j) Visually examine the fuel nozzle wear sleeves.
1) If the wear sleeves are dislodged, they are not serviceable.
a) Replace the fuel nozzles.
b) Use a borescope to examine the combustion chamber for downstream
damage (TASK 72-00-00-200-805-F00) or continue-in-service for five (5)
cycles.
2) If the wear sleeves have four (4) interrupted weld design, they are not serviceable.
a) Replace the fuel nozzles or continue-in-service for five (5) cycles.
(k) Install protective covers on the fuel nozzle, the fuel nozzle port, and the fuel manifold
connection.

E. Fuel Nozzle Installation
SUBTASK 73-11-04-420-038-F02
(1) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle [24] at position 1 or 19 (Figure 401, Figure 402,
Figure 403):
(a) Remove the protective cover from the fuel nozzle, fuel nozzle port, and fuel manifold
connection.
(b) Do these steps to install the new preformed packing [22] and new preformed
packing [26]:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Install the preformed packing [22] on the fuel nozzle [24].
3) Install the preformed packing [26] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Install a new seal [28] into the retainer [29].
(d) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle [24]:
1) Put the retainer [29] on the fuel nozzle port.
2) Put the fuel nozzle [24] into the fuel nozzle port, with the tip forward.
3) Turn the fuel nozzle [24] 180 degrees counterclockwise when you move it on to the
combustion case pad.
4) Move the fuel nozzle [24] aft to engage the tip in the combustion chamber.
(e) Apply graphite mineral vaseline, D00601 [CP2101], or Pure Nickel Special compound,
D00006, to the threads of the bolt [23] and bolts [41].
(f) Put the support bracket [42] in its correct position on the fuel nozzle flange.
73-11-04
Config 2
(g) Install the bolt [23] and bolts [41] in the fuel nozzle flange.
1) Tighten the bolt [23] and bolts [41] to 110 in-lb (12.4 N·m) - 120 in-lb (13.6 N·m).
2) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
bolt [23] and bolts [41].
(h) Install the bolt [44] that holds the loop clamp [43] to the support bracket [42].
1) Tighten the bolt [44] to 62 in-lb (7.0 N·m) - 68 in-lb (7.7 N·m).
(i) Do these steps to install the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21] to the fuel
nozzle [24]:
1) Connect the coupling nut [25] to the nipple on the fuel nozzle [24].
a) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
2) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
3) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
4) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
5) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
6) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
coupling nut [25].
(j) Attach the shroud [27] to the fuel nozzle [24], do these steps:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Slide the shroud [27] over the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21].
3) Tighten the shroud [27] on the fuel nozzle until no more than two threads are seen.
4) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
shroud [27].
(k) For the fuel nozzle position 1, do this task: Bleed Air Precooler Reconnection (After
Engine Component Installation), TASK 36-12-01-400-801.
SUBTASK 73-11-04-420-039-F02
(2) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle [24] at position 2, 4, 6, 10, 14, 16, or 20 (Figure 401,
Figure 402, Figure 404):
(a) Remove the protective cover from the fuel nozzle, fuel nozzle port, and fuel manifold
connection.
(b) Do these steps to install the new preformed packing [22] and new preformed
packing [26]:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Install the preformed packing [22] on the fuel nozzle [24].
3) Install the preformed packing [26] on the fuel manifold [21].
(c) Install a new seal [28] into the retainer [29].
(d) Do these steps to install the fuel nozzle [24]:
1) Put the retainer [29] on the fuel nozzle port.
2) Put the fuel nozzle [24] into the fuel nozzle port, with the tip forward.
3) Turn the fuel nozzle [24] counter-clockwise when you move it on to the combustion
case pad.
73-11-04
Config 2
4) Move the fuel nozzle [24] aft to engage the tip in the combustion chamber.
(e) Apply graphite mineral vaseline, D00601 [CP2101], or Pure Nickel Special compound,
D00006, to the threads of the bolt [23] and bolts [41].
(f) Move the hinged bracket [61] into its position on the fuel nozzle flange.
(g) Install the bolt [23] and bolts [41] in the fuel nozzle flange.
1) Tighten the bolt [23] and bolts [41] to 110 in-lb (12.4 N·m) - 120 in-lb (13.6 N·m).
2) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
bolt [23] and bolts [41].
(h) Do these steps to install the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21] to the fuel
nozzle [24]:
1) Connect the coupling nut [25] to the nipple on the fuel nozzle [24].
a) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
2) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
3) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
4) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
5) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
6) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
coupling nut [25].
(i) Attach the shroud [27] to the fuel nozzle [24]:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Slide the shroud [27] over the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21].
3) Tighten the shroud [27] on the fuel nozzle until no more than two threads are seen.
4) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
shroud [27].
(j) For the fuel nozzle positions 2 and 20, do this task: Bleed Air Precooler Reconnection
(After Engine Component Installation), TASK 36-12-01-400-801.
(k) For the fuel nozzle position 14, install the transient bleed air tube if it is removed.

73-11-04
Config 2

(i) Do these steps to install the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21] to the fuel
nozzle [24]:
1) Connect the coupling nut [25] to the nipple on the fuel nozzle [24].
a) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
2) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
3) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
4) Loosen the coupling nut [25].
5) Tighten the coupling nut [25] to 140 in-lb (16 N·m) - 150 in-lb (17 N·m).
6) Install safety wire, G02345 [CP8001], or safety cable, G50065 [CP8006], on the
coupling nut [25].
(j) Attach the shroud [27] to the fuel nozzle [24]:
1) Apply vaseline, D00672 [CP5070], on the preformed packing [22] and preformed
packing [26].
2) Slide the shroud [27] over the coupling nut [25] of the fuel manifold [21].

73-11-04

1) Install a DO-NOT-OPERATE tag on the applicable start lever.

NOTE: The light for the engine fuel shutoff valve has three positions: 1) bright when the
